input.command:
python3 - <<'PY'
import glob, re

files = sorted(glob.glob('characters/*.txt'))
print(len(files), "files")

# Parse blocks with brace matching, reading as windows-1252
blood = []
for f in files:
    raw = open(f, encoding='windows-1252').read()
    # find top-level blocks: start of line, digits, =, {
    starts = [(m.start(), m.group(1)) for m in re.finditer(r'(?m)^(\d+)\s*=\s*\{', raw)]
    print(f, len(starts), "blocks")
    for idx, (pos, cid) in enumerate(starts):
        end = starts[idx+1][0] if idx+1 < len(starts) else len(raw)
        block = raw[pos:end]
        # trim to balanced braces: find first { then match
        bi = block.index('{')
        depth = 0
        cut = None
        for i, ch in enumerate(block[bi:], start=bi):
            if ch == '{': depth += 1
            elif ch == '}':
                depth -= 1
                if depth == 0:
                    cut = i+1
                    break
        if cut is None:
            print("  UNBALANCED", cid)
            continue
        body = block[:cut]
        if 'create_bloodline' in body:
            # find bloodline types
            types = re.findall(r'create_bloodline\s*=\s*\{([^}]*)\}', body)
            blood.append((f, cid, types, body))

print("===")
print(len(blood), "bloodline characters")
for f, cid, types, body in blood:
    t = [re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', x).strip() for x in types]
    print(f, cid, t)
PY

call_01a096bb9dfb7b629aea7dcef074ef7f

input.command:
python3 - <<'PY'
import glob, re, os

SRC = 'characters'
OUT = 'character_sheets'
os.makedirs(OUT, exist_ok=True)

def strip_comments(s):
    """Replace '#...' comments with spaces (keep offsets, newlines, quotes aware)."""
    out = []
    i, n = 0, len(s)
    in_str = False
    while i < n:
        c = s[i]
        if in_str:
            out.append(c)
            if c == '"':
                in_str = False
            i += 1
        elif c == '"':
            in_str = True
            out.append(c)
            i += 1
        elif c == '#':
            while i < n and s[i] not in '\r\n':
                out.append(' ')
                i += 1
        else:
            out.append(c)
            i += 1
    return ''.join(out)

def match_brace(s, open_pos):
    depth = 0
    for i in range(open_pos, len(s)):
        if s[i] == '{':
            depth += 1
        elif s[i] == '}':
            depth -= 1
            if depth == 0:
                return i + 1
    return None

def clean_val(v):
    v = v.strip()
    if len(v) >= 2 and v.startswith('"') and v.endswith('"'):
        return v[1:-1]
    return v

def norm(s):
    return s.replace('\r\n', '\n').replace('\r', '\n')

def tok_re(key):
    return re.compile(r'(?<![A-Za-z_])' + key + r'\s*=\s*("[^"]*"|[^\s\}]+)')

def get_tok(base, key):
    m = tok_re(key).search(base)
    return m.group(1).strip() if m else None

# ---------- parse every history file ----------
files = sorted(glob.glob(f'{SRC}/*.txt'))
all_blocks = {}
dups = []
for f in files:
    raw = open(f, encoding='windows-1252').read()
    st = strip_comments(raw)
    starts = [(m.start(), m.group(1)) for m in re.finditer(r'(?m)^(\d+)\s*=\s*\{', st)]
    for pos, cid in starts:
        ob = st.index('{', pos)
        end = match_brace(st, ob)
        if end is None:
            print('UNBALANCED', f, cid)
            continue
        line = raw.count('\n', 0, pos) + 1
        rec = {'file': f, 'raw': raw[pos:end], 'st': st[pos:end], 'line': line}
        if cid in all_blocks:
            dups.append((cid, all_blocks[cid]['file'], f))
        else:
            all_blocks[cid] = rec
print('blocks:', len(all_blocks), 'dups:', dups)

date_re = re.compile(r'(?m)^[ \t]*(\d{1,4}\.\d{1,2}\.\d{1,2})\s*=\s*\{')

def date_spans(body_st):
    spans = []
    for m in date_re.finditer(body_st):
        ob = body_st.index('{', m.start())
        end = match_brace(body_st, ob)
        if end is None:
            continue
        spans.append((m.group(1), m.start(), end))
    return spans

def mask_spans(s, spans):
    buf = list(s)
    for _, a, b in spans:
        for i in range(a, b):
            if buf[i] not in '\r\n':
                buf[i] = ' '
    return ''.join(buf)

name_index = {}
for cid, r in all_blocks.items():
    t = get_tok(r['st'], 'name')
    name_index[cid] = clean_val(t) if t else '(no name= line)'

# children reverse index (father=/mother= anywhere = top level in practice)
children = {}
for cid, r in all_blocks.items():
    for m in re.finditer(r'(?<![A-Za-z_])(father|mother)\s*=\s*(\d+)', r['st']):
        children.setdefault(m.group(2), []).append((cid, m.group(1)))

def first_birth_death(cid):
    r = all_blocks.get(cid)
    if not r:
        return None, None
    birth = death = None
    for _d, a, b in date_spans(r['st']):
        inner = r['st'][a:b]
        if birth is None:
            m = tok_re('birth').search(inner)
            if m:
                birth = clean_val(m.group(1))
        if death is None:
            m = re.search(r'(?<![A-Za-z_])death\s*=\s*(\{[^{}]*\}|"[^"]*"|[^\s\}]+)', inner, re.S)
            if m:
                dv = m.group(1)
                if dv.startswith('{'):
                    dv = re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', dv).strip()
                death = clean_val(dv)
        if birth and death:
            break
    return birth, death

def person_ref(pid):
    if pid in all_blocks:
        return f'`{pid}` — {name_index[pid]} (`{all_blocks[pid]["file"]}`)'
    return f'`{pid}` — (ID not found in these history files)'

summ_re = re.compile(
    r'(?<![A-Za-z_])(birth|death|trait|add_trait|remove_trait|give_nickname|add_spouse|remove_spouse|'
    r'add_claim|create_bloodline|prestige|piety|religion|culture|dynasty|employer|name|'
    r'give_job_title|set_government_type)\s*=\s*(\{[^{}]*\}|"[^"]*"|[^\s\}]+)', re.S)

def summarize(inner_st):
    parts = []
    for m in summ_re.finditer(inner_st):
        v = re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', m.group(2)).strip()
        parts.append(f'{m.group(1)}={v}')
    return '; '.join(parts) if parts else '(see verbatim block below)'

KNOWN_BASE_KEYS = {'name','female','dynasty','religion','culture','martial','diplomacy',
    'intrigue','stewardship','learning','dna','properties','father','mother','trait','add_trait'}

blood = [(cid, r) for cid, r in all_blocks.items() if 'create_bloodline' in r['st']]
blood.sort(key=lambda x: (x[1]['file'], int(x[0])))
print('bloodline holders:', len(blood))

def md_cell(s):
    return str(s).replace('|', '\\|').replace('\n', ' ')

sheets = []
for cid, r in blood:
    body_st, body_raw = r['st'], r['raw']
    spans = date_spans(body_st)
    base = mask_spans(body_st, spans)

    name_tok = get_tok(base, 'name')
    name = clean_val(name_tok) if name_tok else '(no name= line)'
    female_tok = get_tok(base, 'female')
    dynasty_tok = get_tok(base, 'dynasty')
    religion_tok = get_tok(base, 'religion')
    culture_tok = get_tok(base, 'culture')
    father_tok = get_tok(base, 'father')
    mother_tok = get_tok(base, 'mother')
    dna_tok = get_tok(base, 'dna')
    prop_tok = get_tok(base, 'properties')
    attrs = {k: get_tok(base, k) for k in ('martial','diplomacy','intrigue','stewardship','learning')}
    traits = [m.group(1).strip() for m in re.finditer(
        r'(?<![A-Za-z_])(?:add_trait|trait)\s*=\s*("[^"]*"|[^\s\}]+)', base)]

    # bloodlines with dates + raw snippets
    blines = []
    for d, a, b in spans:
        inner_st = body_st[a:b]
        for m in re.finditer(r'(?<![A-Za-z_])create_bloodline\s*=\s*\{', inner_st):
            ob = inner_st.index('{', m.start())
            end = match_brace(inner_st, ob)
            inner = inner_st[ob:end]
            tm = re.search(r'(?<![A-Za-z_])type\s*=\s*([A-Za-z0-9_]+)', inner)
            btype = tm.group(1) if tm else '(no type= found)'
            # raw snippet, same offsets in raw body
            g_ob = a + m.start()
            line_start = body_raw.rfind('\n', 0, g_ob) + 1
            raw_snip = norm(body_raw[line_start:a+end])
            blines.append((d, btype, raw_snip))
    # (fallback) bloodline outside date blocks
    if not blines:
        for m in re.finditer(r'(?<![A-Za-z_])create_bloodline\s*=\s*\{', base):
            ob = base.index('{', m.start())
            end = match_brace(base, ob)
            inner = base[ob:end]
            tm = re.search(r'(?<![A-Za-z_])type\s*=\s*([A-Za-z0-9_]+)', inner)
            btype = tm.group(1) if tm else '(no type= found)'
            line_start = body_raw.rfind('\n', 0, m.start()) + 1
            blines.append(('(top level, no date)', btype, norm(body_raw[line_start:end])))

    slug_types = '_'.join(re.sub(r'\W+', '', t) for _, t, _ in blines) or 'bloodline'
    fname = f'{cid}_{slug_types}.md'

    # spouses dated
    spouses = []
    for d, a, b in spans:
        inner = body_st[a:b]
        for m in re.finditer(r'(?<![A-Za-z_])(add_spouse|remove_spouse)\s*=\s*(\d+)', inner):
            spouses.append((d, m.group(1), m.group(2)))

    # children
    kids = []
    for kcid, role in sorted(children.get(cid, []), key=lambda x: int(x[0])):
        kb, _kd = first_birth_death(kcid)
        kids.append((kcid, role, kb))

    # chronology + verbatim dates
    chrono = []
    verbatim_dates = []
    for d, a, b in spans:
        chrono.append((d, summarize(body_st[a:b])))
        verbatim_dates.append((d, norm(body_raw[a:b]).rstrip()))

    birth, death = first_birth_death(cid)

    # other top-level keys (extras beyond standard set)
    base_keys = set(re.findall(r'(?m)^[ \t]*([A-Za-z_]+)\s*=', base))
    extras = sorted(k for k in base_keys if k not in KNOWN_BASE_KEYS and k != '')

    # ---- build markdown ----
    L = []
    L.append(f'# {name} — character {cid}')
    L.append('')
    L.append('> Transcribed as-is from the history files. Nothing invented; values below are the exact tokens from the source block.')
    L.append(f'> Source: `{r["file"]}` (line {r["line"]}), block `{cid} = {{ ... }}`.')
    L.append('> Display only: line endings normalized (CRLF → LF), encoding converted (Windows-1252 → UTF-8). Inline `#` comments are preserved in the verbatim sections.')
    L.append('')
    L.append('## Identity (as in file)')
    L.append('')
    L.append('| Field | Value in file |')
    L.append('|---|---|')
    L.append(f'| Character ID | `{cid}` |')
    L.append(f'| Name | `{md_cell(name_tok)}` |' if name_tok else '| Name | — (absent) |')
    if female_tok and female_tok.lower() == 'yes':
        L.append('| Sex | female (`female = yes`) |')
    elif female_tok:
        L.append(f'| Sex | `{md_cell(female_tok)}` (unusual `female=` value — see verbatim) |')
    else:
        L.append('| Sex | male (default — no `female=` line) |')
    L.append(f'| Dynasty | `{md_cell(dynasty_tok)}` |' if dynasty_tok else '| Dynasty | — (absent; lowborn / no dynasty line) |')
    L.append(f'| Religion | `{md_cell(religion_tok)}` |' if religion_tok else '| Religion | — (absent) |')
    L.append(f'| Culture | `{md_cell(culture_tok)}` |' if culture_tok else '| Culture | — (absent) |')
    L.append(f'| Father | {person_ref(father_tok)} |' if father_tok else '| Father | — (no `father=` line) |')
    L.append(f'| Mother | {person_ref(mother_tok)} |' if mother_tok else '| Mother | — (no `mother=` line) |')
    L.append(f'| DNA | `{md_cell(dna_tok)}` |' if dna_tok else '| DNA | — (absent) |')
    L.append(f'| Properties | `{md_cell(prop_tok)}` |' if prop_tok else '| Properties | — (absent) |')
    L.append('')
    L.append('## Attributes (as in file)')
    L.append('')
    L.append('| martial | diplomacy | intrigue | stewardship | learning |')
    L.append('|---|---|---|---|---|')
    L.append('| ' + ' | '.join(f'`{attrs[k]}`' if attrs[k] else '—' for k in ('martial','diplomacy','intrigue','stewardship','learning')) + ' |')
    L.append('')
    L.append('## Traits (base entries, as in file)')
    L.append('')
    if traits:
        for t in traits:
            L.append(f'- `{md_cell(t)}`')
    else:
        L.append('_None at top level._')
    L.append('')
    if extras:
        L.append('## Other top-level fields (as in file)')
        L.append('')
        for k in extras:
            for m in re.finditer(r'(?m)^[ \t]*' + re.escape(k) + r'\s*=\s*("[^"]*"|[^\s\}]+|\{)', base):
                L.append(f'- `{md_cell(k)} = `{md_cell(m.group(1))}`' if not m.group(1).startswith('{') else f'- `{md_cell(k)} = {{ ... }}` (see verbatim block)')
        L.append('')
    L.append('## Bloodline(s) (as in file)')
    L.append('')
    for d, t, snip in blines:
        L.append(f'### `{t}` — granted {d}')
        L.append('')
        L.append('```text')
        L.append(snip)
        L.append('```')
        L.append('')
    L.append('## Family & relations (IDs as in file; names looked up from the same history files)')
    L.append('')
    L.append(f'- Father: {person_ref(father_tok)}' if father_tok else '- Father: — (no `father=` line)')
    L.append(f'- Mother: {person_ref(mother_tok)}' if mother_tok else '- Mother: — (no `mother=` line)')
    if spouses:
        L.append('- Spouses:')
        for d, act, pid in spouses:
            verb = 'added' if act == 'add_spouse' else 'removed'
            L.append(f'  - {verb} {d}: {person_ref(pid)}')
    else:
        L.append('- Spouses: — (no `add_spouse=` / `remove_spouse=` lines)')
    if kids:
        L.append(f'- Recorded children ({len(kids)} other block(s) with `father={cid}` or `mother={cid}`):')
        for kcid, role, kb in kids:
            btxt = f', born {kb}' if kb else ''
            L.append(f'  - `{role}={cid}`: {person_ref(kcid)}{btxt}')
    else:
        L.append(f'- Recorded children: — (no block has `father={cid}` or `mother={cid}`)')
    L.append('')
    L.append('## Chronology (date order as in file; statements verbatim)')
    L.append('')
    if chrono:
        for d, s in chrono:
            L.append(f'- **{d}** — `{md_cell(s)}`')
    else:
        L.append('_No dated history entries._')
    L.append('')
    L.append('## History entries (verbatim)')
    L.append('')
    if verbatim_dates:
        for d, rawv in verbatim_dates:
            L.append(f'### {d}')
            L.append('')
            L.append('```text')
            L.append(rawv)
            L.append('```')
            L.append('')
    else:
        L.append('_No dated history entries._')
        L.append('')
    L.append('## Full character block (verbatim)')
    L.append('')
    L.append('```text')
    L.append(norm(body_raw).rstrip())
    L.append('```')
    L.append('')

    open(os.path.join(OUT, fname), 'w', encoding='utf-8', newline='\n').write('\n'.join(L))
    sheets.append({'file': fname, 'cid': cid, 'name': name, 'src': r['file'],
                   'types': ', '.join(t for _, t, _ in blines),
                   'birth': birth or '—', 'death': death or '—',
                   'culture': clean_val(culture_tok) if culture_tok else '—',
                   'religion': clean_val(religion_tok) if religion_tok else '—',
                   'dynasty': dynasty_tok or '—'})

# ---------- index ----------
idx = []
idx.append('# Character sheets — bloodline holders')
idx.append('')
idx.append(f'{len(sheets)} bloodline holders transcribed as-is from `characters/*.txt` (every block containing `create_bloodline`). No values invented.')
idx.append('')
idx.append('Notes:')
idx.append('')
idx.append('- Each sheet quotes its source block verbatim, plus field-by-field tables of the exact tokens in the file.')
idx.append('- Display only: CRLF → LF line endings, Windows-1252 → UTF-8. Inline `#` comments appear in the verbatim sections.')
idx.append('- Dynasty names are NOT in this repo (only numeric `dynasty=` IDs), so sheets list the ID without inventing a name.')
idx.append('- Father / mother / spouse / children names are looked up from these same history files by character ID and labelled as such.')
idx.append('')
idx.append('| # | Character | ID | Source file | Bloodline | Born | Died |')
idx.append('|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|')
for i, s in enumerate(sheets, 1):
    idx.append(f'| {i} | [{md_cell(s["name"])}](./{s["file"]}) | `{s["cid"]}` | `{s["src"]}` | `{md_cell(s["types"])}` | {md_cell(s["birth"])} | {md_cell(s["death"])} |')
idx.append('')
open(os.path.join(OUT, 'README.md'), 'w', encoding='utf-8', newline='\n').write('\n'.join(idx))
print('wrote', len(sheets), 'sheets + README')

input.command:
python3 - <<'PY'
import glob, re, os

SRC = 'characters'
OUT = 'character_sheets'
os.makedirs(OUT, exist_ok=True)

def strip_comments(s):
    out = []
    i, n = 0, len(s)
    in_str = False
    while i < n:
        c = s[i]
        if in_str:
            out.append(c)
            if c == '"':
                in_str = False
            i += 1
        elif c == '"':
            in_str = True
            out.append(c)
            i += 1
        elif c == '#':
            while i < n and s[i] not in '\r\n':
                out.append(' ')
                i += 1
        else:
            out.append(c)
            i += 1
    return ''.join(out)

def match_brace(s, open_pos):
    depth = 0
    for i in range(open_pos, len(s)):
        if s[i] == '{':
            depth += 1
        elif s[i] == '}':
            depth -= 1
            if depth == 0:
                return i + 1
    return None

def clean_val(v):
    v = v.strip()
    if len(v) >= 2 and v.startswith('"') and v.endswith('"'):
        return v[1:-1]
    return v

def norm(s):
    return s.replace('\r\n', '\n').replace('\r', '\n')

def tok_re(key):
    return re.compile(r'(?<![A-Za-z_])' + key + r'\s*=\s*("[^"]*"|[^\s\}]+)')

def get_tok(base, key):
    m = tok_re(key).search(base)
    return m.group(1).strip() if m else None

files = sorted(glob.glob(f'{SRC}/*.txt'))
all_blocks = {}
dups = []
for f in files:
    raw = open(f, encoding='windows-1252').read()
    st = strip_comments(raw)
    starts = [(m.start(), m.group(1)) for m in re.finditer(r'(?m)^(\d+)\s*=\s*\{', st)]
    for pos, cid in starts:
        ob = st.index('{', pos)
        end = match_brace(st, ob)
        if end is None:
            print('UNBALANCED', f, cid)
            continue
        line = raw.count('\n', 0, pos) + 1
        rec = {'file': f, 'raw': raw[pos:end], 'st': st[pos:end], 'line': line}
        if cid in all_blocks:
            dups.append((cid, all_blocks[cid]['file'], f))
        else:
            all_blocks[cid] = rec
print('blocks:', len(all_blocks), 'dups:', dups)

date_re = re.compile(r'(?m)^[ \t]*(\d{1,4}\.\d{1,2}\.\d{1,2})\s*=\s*\{')

def date_spans(body_st):
    spans = []
    for m in date_re.finditer(body_st):
        ob = body_st.index('{', m.start())
        end = match_brace(body_st, ob)
        if end is None:
            continue
        spans.append((m.group(1), m.start(), end))
    return spans

def mask_spans(s, spans):
    buf = list(s)
    for _, a, b in spans:
        for i in range(a, b):
            if buf[i] not in '\r\n':
                buf[i] = ' '
    return ''.join(buf)

name_index = {}
for cid, r in all_blocks.items():
    t = get_tok(r['st'], 'name')
    name_index[cid] = clean_val(t) if t else '(no name= line)'

children = {}
for cid, r in all_blocks.items():
    for m in re.finditer(r'(?<![A-Za-z_])(father|mother)\s*=\s*(\d+)', r['st']):
        children.setdefault(m.group(2), []).append((cid, m.group(1)))

def first_birth_death(cid):
    """Return (birth_display, death_display). When value is `yes`, show the entry's date key (also from file)."""
    r = all_blocks.get(cid)
    if not r:
        return None, None
    birth = death = None
    for d, a, b in date_spans(r['st']):
        inner = r['st'][a:b]
        if birth is None:
            m = tok_re('birth').search(inner)
            if m:
                v = clean_val(m.group(1))
                birth = d if v.lower() == 'yes' else v
        if death is None:
            m = re.search(r'(?<![A-Za-z_])death\s*=\s*(\{[^{}]*\}|"[^"]*"|[^\s\}]+)', inner, re.S)
            if m:
                dv = m.group(1)
                if dv.startswith('{'):
                    dv = re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', dv).strip()
                else:
                    dv = clean_val(dv)
                    if dv.lower() == 'yes':
                        dv = d
                death = dv
        if birth and death:
            break
    return birth, death

def person_ref(pid):
    if pid in all_blocks:
        return f'`{pid}` — {name_index[pid]} (`{all_blocks[pid]["file"]}`)'
    return f'`{pid}` — (ID not found in these history files)'

summ_re = re.compile(
    r'(?<![A-Za-z_])(birth|death|trait|add_trait|remove_trait|give_nickname|add_spouse|remove_spouse|'
    r'add_claim|create_bloodline|prestige|piety|religion|culture|dynasty|employer|name|'
    r'give_job_title|set_government_type)\s*=\s*(\{[^{}]*\}|"[^"]*"|[^\s\}]+)', re.S)

def summarize(inner_st):
    parts = []
    for m in summ_re.finditer(inner_st):
        v = re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', m.group(2)).strip()
        parts.append(f'{m.group(1)}={v}')
    return '; '.join(parts) if parts else '(see verbatim block below)'

KNOWN_BASE_KEYS = {'name','female','dynasty','religion','culture','martial','diplomacy',
    'intrigue','stewardship','learning','dna','properties','father','mother','trait','add_trait'}

blood = [(cid, r) for cid, r in all_blocks.items() if 'create_bloodline' in r['st']]
blood.sort(key=lambda x: (x[1]['file'], int(x[0])))
print('bloodline holders:', len(blood))

def md_cell(s):
    return str(s).replace('|', '\\|').replace('\n', ' ')

sheets = []
for cid, r in blood:
    body_st, body_raw = r['st'], r['raw']
    spans = date_spans(body_st)
    base = mask_spans(body_st, spans)

    name_tok = get_tok(base, 'name')
    name = clean_val(name_tok) if name_tok else '(no name= line)'
    female_tok = get_tok(base, 'female')
    dynasty_tok = get_tok(base, 'dynasty')
    religion_tok = get_tok(base, 'religion')
    culture_tok = get_tok(base, 'culture')
    father_tok = get_tok(base, 'father')
    mother_tok = get_tok(base, 'mother')
    dna_tok = get_tok(base, 'dna')
    prop_tok = get_tok(base, 'properties')
    attrs = {k: get_tok(base, k) for k in ('martial','diplomacy','intrigue','stewardship','learning')}
    traits = [m.group(1).strip() for m in re.finditer(
        r'(?<![A-Za-z_])(?:add_trait|trait)\s*=\s*("[^"]*"|[^\s\}]+)', base)]

    blines = []
    for d, a, b in spans:
        inner_st = body_st[a:b]
        for m in re.finditer(r'(?<![A-Za-z_])create_bloodline\s*=\s*\{', inner_st):
            ob = inner_st.index('{', m.start())
            end = match_brace(inner_st, ob)
            inner = inner_st[ob:end]
            tm = re.search(r'(?<![A-Za-z_])type\s*=\s*([A-Za-z0-9_]+)', inner)
            btype = tm.group(1) if tm else '(no type= found)'
            g_ob = a + m.start()
            line_start = body_raw.rfind('\n', 0, g_ob) + 1
            raw_snip = norm(body_raw[line_start:a+end])
            blines.append((d, btype, raw_snip))
    if not blines:
        for m in re.finditer(r'(?<![A-Za-z_])create_bloodline\s*=\s*\{', base):
            ob = base.index('{', m.start())
            end = match_brace(base, ob)
            inner = base[ob:end]
            tm = re.search(r'(?<![A-Za-z_])type\s*=\s*([A-Za-z0-9_]+)', inner)
            btype = tm.group(1) if tm else '(no type= found)'
            line_start = body_raw.rfind('\n', 0, m.start()) + 1
            blines.append(('(top level, no date)', btype, norm(body_raw[line_start:end])))

    slug_types = '_'.join(re.sub(r'\W+', '', t) for _, t, _ in blines) or 'bloodline'
    fname = f'{cid}_{slug_types}.md'

    spouses = []
    for d, a, b in spans:
        inner = body_st[a:b]
        for m in re.finditer(r'(?<![A-Za-z_])(add_spouse|remove_spouse)\s*=\s*(\d+)', inner):
            spouses.append((d, m.group(1), m.group(2)))

    kids = []
    for kcid, role in sorted(children.get(cid, []), key=lambda x: int(x[0])):
        kb, _kd = first_birth_death(kcid)
        kids.append((kcid, role, kb))

    chrono = []
    verbatim_dates = []
    for d, a, b in spans:
        chrono.append((d, summarize(body_st[a:b])))
        verbatim_dates.append((d, norm(body_raw[a:b]).rstrip()))

    birth, death = first_birth_death(cid)

    base_keys = set(re.findall(r'(?m)^[ \t]*([A-Za-z_]+)\s*=', base))
    extras = sorted(k for k in base_keys if k not in KNOWN_BASE_KEYS and k != '')

    L = []
    L.append(f'# {name} — character {cid}')
    L.append('')
    L.append('> Transcribed as-is from the history files. Nothing invented; values below are the exact tokens from the source block.')
    L.append(f'> Source: `{r["file"]}` (line {r["line"]}), block `{cid} = {{ ... }}`.')
    L.append('> Display only: line endings normalized (CRLF → LF), encoding converted (Windows-1252 → UTF-8). Inline `#` comments are preserved in the verbatim sections.')
    L.append('')
    L.append('## Identity (as in file)')
    L.append('')
    L.append('| Field | Value in file |')
    L.append('|---|---|')
    L.append(f'| Character ID | `{cid}` |')
    L.append(f'| Name | `{md_cell(name_tok)}` |' if name_tok else '| Name | — (absent) |')
    if female_tok and female_tok.lower() == 'yes':
        L.append('| Sex | female (`female = yes`) |')
    elif female_tok:
        L.append(f'| Sex | `{md_cell(female_tok)}` (unusual `female=` value — see verbatim) |')
    else:
        L.append('| Sex | male (default — no `female=` line) |')
    L.append(f'| Dynasty | `{md_cell(dynasty_tok)}` |' if dynasty_tok else '| Dynasty | — (absent; lowborn / no dynasty line) |')
    L.append(f'| Religion | `{md_cell(religion_tok)}` |' if religion_tok else '| Religion | — (absent) |')
    L.append(f'| Culture | `{md_cell(culture_tok)}` |' if culture_tok else '| Culture | — (absent) |')
    L.append(f'| Father | {person_ref(father_tok)} |' if father_tok else '| Father | — (no `father=` line) |')
    L.append(f'| Mother | {person_ref(mother_tok)} |' if mother_tok else '| Mother | — (no `mother=` line) |')
    L.append(f'| DNA | `{md_cell(dna_tok)}` |' if dna_tok else '| DNA | — (absent) |')
    L.append(f'| Properties | `{md_cell(prop_tok)}` |' if prop_tok else '| Properties | — (absent) |')
    L.append('')
    L.append('## Attributes (as in file)')
    L.append('')
    L.append('| martial | diplomacy | intrigue | stewardship | learning |')
    L.append('|---|---|---|---|---|')
    L.append('| ' + ' | '.join(f'`{attrs[k]}`' if attrs[k] else '—' for k in ('martial','diplomacy','intrigue','stewardship','learning')) + ' |')
    L.append('')
    L.append('## Traits (base entries, as in file)')
    L.append('')
    if traits:
        for t in traits:
            L.append(f'- `{md_cell(t)}`')
    else:
        L.append('_None at top level._')
    L.append('')
    if extras:
        L.append('## Other top-level fields (as in file)')
        L.append('')
        for k in extras:
            for m in re.finditer(r'(?m)^[ \t]*' + re.escape(k) + r'\s*=\s*("[^"]*"|[^\s\}]+|\{)', base):
            #     ^^^ keep loose: value token or block start
                v = m.group(1)
                if v.startswith('{'):
                    L.append(f'- `{k} = {{ ... }}` (see verbatim block)')
                else:
                    L.append(f'- `{k} = {v}`')
        L.append('')
    L.append('## Bloodline(s) (as in file)')
    L.append('')
    for d, t, snip in blines:
        L.append(f'### `{t}` — granted {d}')
        L.append('')
        L.append('```text')
        L.append(snip)
        L.append('```')
        L.append('')
    L.append('## Family & relations (IDs as in file; names looked up from the same history files)')
    L.append('')
    L.append(f'- Father: {person_ref(father_tok)}' if father_tok else '- Father: — (no `father=` line)')
    L.append(f'- Mother: {person_ref(mother_tok)}' if mother_tok else '- Mother: — (no `mother=` line)')
    if spouses:
        L.append('- Spouses:')
        for d, act, pid in spouses:
            verb = 'added' if act == 'add_spouse' else 'removed'
            L.append(f'  - {verb} {d}: {person_ref(pid)}')
    else:
        L.append('- Spouses: — (no `add_spouse=` / `remove_spouse=` lines)')
    if kids:
        L.append(f'- Recorded children ({len(kids)} other block(s) with `father={cid}` or `mother={cid}`):')
        for kcid, role, kb in kids:
            btxt = f', born {kb}' if kb else ''
            L.append(f'  - `{role}={cid}`: {person_ref(kcid)}{btxt}')
    else:
        L.append(f'- Recorded children: — (no block has `father={cid}` or `mother={cid}`)')
    L.append('')
    L.append('## Chronology (date order as in file; statements verbatim)')
    L.append('')
    if chrono:
        for d, s in chrono:
            L.append(f'- **{d}** — `{md_cell(s)}`')
    else:
        L.append('_No dated history entries._')
    L.append('')
    L.append('## History entries (verbatim)')
    L.append('')
    if verbatim_dates:
        for d, rawv in verbatim_dates:
            L.append(f'### {d}')
            L.append('')
            L.append('```text')
            L.append(rawv)
            L.append('```')
            L.append('')
    else:
        L.append('_No dated history entries._')
        L.append('')
    L.append('## Full character block (verbatim)')
    L.append('')
    L.append('```text')
    L.append(norm(body_raw).rstrip())
    L.append('```')
    L.append('')

    open(os.path.join(OUT, fname), 'w', encoding='utf-8', newline='\n').write('\n'.join(L))
    sheets.append({'file': fname, 'cid': cid, 'name': name, 'src': r['file'],
                   'types': ', '.join(t for _, t, _ in blines),
                   'birth': birth or '—', 'death': death or '—',
                   'culture': clean_val(culture_tok) if culture_tok else '—',
                   'religion': clean_val(religion_tok) if religion_tok else '—',
                   'dynasty': dynasty_tok or '—'})

idx = []
idx.append('# Character sheets — bloodline holders')
idx.append('')
idx.append(f'{len(sheets)} bloodline holders transcribed as-is from `characters/*.txt` (every block containing `create_bloodline`). No values invented.')
idx.append('')
idx.append('Notes:')
idx.append('')
idx.append('- Each sheet quotes its source block verbatim, plus field-by-field tables of the exact tokens in the file.')
idx.append('- Display only: CRLF → LF line endings, Windows-1252 → UTF-8. Inline `#` comments appear in the verbatim sections.')
idx.append('- Dynasty names are NOT in this repo (only numeric `dynasty=` IDs), so sheets list the ID without inventing a name.')
idx.append('- Father / mother / spouse / children names are looked up from these same history files by character ID and labelled as such.')
idx.append('- Where the file says `birth=yes` / `death=yes`, the Born/Died columns show that entry\'s date key (also from the file); the verbatim sections keep the raw `yes`.')
idx.append('')
idx.append('| # | Character | ID | Source file | Bloodline | Born | Died |')
idx.append('|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|')
for i, s in enumerate(sheets, 1):
    idx.append(f'| {i} | [{md_cell(s["name"])}](./{s["file"]}) | `{s["cid"]}` | `{s["src"]}` | `{md_cell(s["types"])}` | {md_cell(s["birth"])} | {md_cell(s["death"])} |')
idx.append('')
open(os.path.join(OUT, 'README.md'), 'w', encoding='utf-8', newline='\n').write('\n'.join(idx))
print('wrote', len(sheets), 'sheets + README')

# Character sheets — bloodline holders

62 bloodline holders transcribed as-is from `characters/*.txt` (every block containing `create_bloodline`). No values invented.

import glob, re
from collections import Counter, defaultdict

def strip_comments(s):
    out=[]; i,n=0,len(s); ins=False
    while i<n:
        c=s[i]
        if ins:
            out.append(c)
            if c=='"': ins=False
            i+=1
        elif c=='"':
            ins=True; out.append(c); i+=1
        elif c=='#':
            while i<n and s[i] not in '\r\n': out.append(' '); i+=1
        else:
            out.append(c); i+=1
    return ''.join(out)

def match_brace(s,p):
    d=0
    for i in range(p,len(s)):
        if s[i]=='{': d+=1
        elif s[i]=='}':
            d-=1
            if d==0: return i+1
    return None

files=sorted(glob.glob('characters/*.txt'))
blocks={}; dups=[]
for f in files:
    raw=open(f,encoding='windows-1252').read()
    st=strip_comments(raw)
    for m in re.finditer(r'(?m)^(\d+)\s*=\s*\{',st):
        pos,cid=m.start(),m.group(1)
        end=match_brace(st,st.index('{',pos))
        if end is None: print('UNBALANCED',f,cid); continue
        if cid in blocks: dups.append((cid,blocks[cid][0],f))
        else: blocks[cid]=(f,st[pos:end])
print('files:',len(files),'blocks:',len(blocks),'dup ids:',len(dups))
for d in dups[:20]: print(' DUP',d)

# reference audit
ref_keys=['father','mother','add_spouse','remove_spouse','employer','killer','add_friend','add_rival','add_lover','add_consort','remove_friend']
refs=defaultdict(list)  # key -> list of (src_cid, src_file, target)
for cid,(f,st) in blocks.items():
    for k in ref_keys:
        for m in re.finditer(r'(?<![A-Za-z_])'+k+r'\s*=\s*(\d+)',st):
            refs[k].append((cid,f,m.group(1)))

print()
print(f"{'key':15} {'total':>7} {'dangling':>9}")
tot_dangle=defaultdict(list)
for k in ref_keys:
    v=refs[k]
    d=[r for r in v if r[2] not in blocks]
    tot_dangle[k]=d
    print(f"{k:15} {len(v):>7} {len(d):>9}")

print()
print('--- dangling father/mother (first 30) ---')
for k in ('father','mother'):
    print(f'## {k}: {len(tot_dangle[k])}')
    for src,f,t in tot_dangle[k][:30]:
        print(f'  {f} char {src} -> missing {k}={t}')

print()
print('--- dangling spouses (first 30) ---')
for k in ('add_spouse','remove_spouse'):
    print(f'## {k}: {len(tot_dangle[k])}')
    for src,f,t in tot_dangle[k][:30]:
        print(f'  {f} char {src} -> missing {k}={t}')

print()
print('--- self refs / father==mother ---')
for cid,(f,st) in blocks.items():
    fm=dict(re.findall(r'(?<![A-Za-z_])(father|mother)\s*=\s*(\d+)',st))
    if fm.get('father')==cid or fm.get('mother')==cid:
        print(' SELF-PARENT',f,cid,fm)
    if 'father' in fm and fm.get('father')==fm.get('mother'):
        print(' FATHER==MOTHER',f,cid,fm)

# id range overlaps: same numeric id used in >1 file would be dup (none). Check numeric ranges per file
print()
print('--- id range per file (min..max, count) ---')
perfile=defaultdict(list)
for cid,(f,st) in blocks.items():
    perfile[f].append(int(cid))
for f in files:
    ids=perfile[f]
    if ids:
        print(f'{f:35} {min(ids):>7}..{max(ids):<7} n={len(ids):>5} uniq={len(set(ids))}')

python3 - <<'PY'
import re
def strip_comments(s):
    out=[]; i,n=0,len(s); ins=False
    while i<n:
        c=s[i]
        if ins:
            out.append(c)
            if c=='"': ins=False
            i+=1
        elif c=='"': ins=True; out.append(c); i+=1
        elif c=='#':
            while i<n and s[i] not in '\r\n': out.append(' '); i+=1
        else: out.append(c); i+=1
    return ''.join(out)
def match_brace(s,p):
    d=0
    for i in range(p,len(s)):
        if s[i]=='{': d+=1
        elif s[i]=='}':
            d-=1
            if d==0: return i+1
    return None
def show(f,cid,context_ids=()):
    raw=open(f,encoding='windows-1252').read()
    st=strip_comments(raw)
    m=re.search(r'(?m)^'+cid+r'\s*=\s*\{',st)
    end=match_brace(st,st.index('{',m.start()))
    print('='*100)
    print(f, 'char', cid)
    print(raw[m.start():end].replace('\r\n','\n'))
    for c in context_ids:
        m2=re.search(r'(?m)^'+c+r'\s*=\s*\{',st)
        if m2:
            e2=match_brace(st,st.index('{',m2.start()))
            print('-'*60); print('context char',c)
            print(raw[m2.start():e2].replace('\r\n','\n'))
        else:
            print('-'*60); print('context char',c,'NOT IN THIS FILE')
show('characters/bedouin_arabic.txt','251001')
show('characters/bedouin_arabic.txt','251004')

import re, glob
def strip_comments(s):
    out=[]; i,n=0,len(s); ins=False
    while i<n:
        c=s[i]
        if ins:
            out.append(c)
            if c=='"': ins=False
            i+=1
        elif c=='"': ins=True; out.append(c); i+=1
        elif c=='#':
            while i<n and s[i] not in '\r\n': out.append(' '); i+=1
        else: out.append(c); i+=1
    return ''.join(out)
def match_brace(s,p):
    d=0
    for i in range(p,len(s)):
        if s[i]=='{': d+=1
        elif s[i]=='}':
            d-=1
            if d==0: return i+1
    return None

# Build full index: cid -> (file, name, dynasty, father, mother)
idx={}
for f in sorted(glob.glob('characters/*.txt')):
    raw=open(f,encoding='windows-1252').read()
    st=strip_comments(raw)
    for m in re.finditer(r'(?m)^(\d+)\s*=\s*\{',st):
        cid=m.group(1)
        end=match_brace(st,st.index('{',m.start()))
        blk=st[m.start():end]
        def g(k):
            mm=re.search(r'(?<![A-Za-z_])'+k+r'\s*=\s*("[^"]*"|[^\s\}]+)',blk)
            return mm.group(1) if mm else None
        idx[cid]=(f,g('name'),g('dynasty'),g('father'),g('mother'))

def show_where(**kw):
    for cid,(f,n,d,fa,mo) in sorted(idx.items(),key=lambda x:int(x[0])):
        ok=True
        for k,v in kw.items():
            actual={'dynasty':d,'father':fa,'mother':mo,'file':f}[k]
            if actual!=v: ok=False
        if ok: print(f'{cid:10} {f:35} name={n} dynasty={d} father={fa} mother={mo}')

print('### dynasty members 1060000/1060001/1060002/1060003 (bedouin fictionals) ###')
for dyn in ('1060000','1060001','1060002','1060003','1060004','1060005'):
    print(f'--- dynasty {dyn} ---')
    show_where(dynasty=dyn)
print()
print('### chars 251001-251006 neighborhood ###')
for c in ('251001','251002','251003','251004','251005','251006'):
    print(c, idx.get(c))
print()
print('### 29100x present? ###')
for c in ('291001','291002','291003','291004','291005'):
    print(c, idx.get(c))

import re
def strip_comments(s):
    out=[]; i,n=0,len(s); ins=False
    while i<n:
        c=s[i]
        if ins:
            out.append(c)
            if c=='"': ins=False
            i+=1
        elif c=='"': ins=True; out.append(c); i+=1
        elif c=='#':
            while i<n and s[i] not in '\r\n': out.append(' '); i+=1
        else: out.append(c); i+=1
    return ''.join(out)
def match_brace(s,p):
    d=0
    for i in range(p,len(s)):
        if s[i]=='{': d+=1
        elif s[i]=='}':
            d-=1
            if d==0: return i+1
    return None
def show(f,cid):
    raw=open(f,encoding='windows-1252').read()
    st=strip_comments(raw)
    m=re.search(r'(?m)^'+cid+r'\s*=\s*\{',st)
    if not m:
        print('='*80); print(f,cid,'MISSING'); return
    end=match_brace(st,st.index('{',m.start()))
    print('='*80); print(f,'char',cid)

import re, glob
def strip_comments(s):
    out=[]; i,n=0,len(s); ins=False
    while i<n:
        c=s[i]
        if ins:
            out.append(c)
            if c=='"': ins=False
            i+=1
        elif c=='"': ins=True; out.append(c); i+=1
        elif c=='#':
            while i<n and s[i] not in '\r\n': out.append(' '); i+=1
        else: out.append(c); i+=1
    return ''.join(out)
def match_brace(s,p):
    d=0
    for i in range(p,len(s)):
        if s[i]=='{': d+=1
        elif s[i]=='}':
            d-=1
            if d==0: return i+1
    return None
idx={}
date_re=re.compile(r'(?m)^[ \t]*(\d{1,4}\.\d{1,2}\.\d{1,2})\s*=\s*\{')
for f in sorted(glob.glob('characters/*.txt')):
    raw=open(f,encoding='windows-1252').read()
    st=strip_comments(raw)
    for m in re.finditer(r'(?m)^(\d+)\s*=\s*\{',st):
        cid=m.group(1)
        end=match_brace(st,st.index('{',m.start()))
        blk=st[m.start():end]
        def g(k):
            mm=re.search(r'(?<![A-Za-z_])'+k+r'\s*=\s*("[^"]*"|[^\s\}]+)',blk)
            return mm.group(1).strip('"') if mm else None
        dates=date_re.findall(blk)
        idx[cid]=(f,g('name'),g('dynasty'),g('father'),g('mother'),dates[0] if dates else None,dates[-1] if dates else None)

def show_where(label,**kw):
    print(f'### {label} ###')
    n=0
    for cid,(f,nm,d,fa,mo,b,dd) in sorted(idx.items(),key=lambda x:int(x[0])):
        m={'dynasty':d,'father':fa,'mother':mo,'file':f}
        if all(m[k]==v for k,v in kw.items()):
            print(f'{cid:10} {f:30} name={nm} dyn={d} fa={fa} mo={mo} first={b} last={dd}'); n+=1
    if n==0: print('  (none)')
    print()

show_where('refs to 251000/251001/251003/251004 as father', father='251000')
show_where('father=251001', father='251001')

            return mm.group(1).strip('"') if mm else None
        dates=date_re.findall(blk)
        idx[cid]=(f,g('name'),g('dynasty'),g('father'),g('mother'),dates[0] if dates else None,dates[-1] if dates else None)
def show_where(label,**kw):
    print(f'### {label} ###')
    n=0
    for cid,(f,nm,d,fa,mo,b,dd) in sorted(idx.items(),key=lambda x:int(x[0])):
        if all({'dynasty':d,'father':fa,'mother':mo,'file':f}[k]==v for k,v in kw.items()):
            print(f'{cid:12} {f:30} name={nm} dyn={d} fa={fa} mo={mo} first={b} last={dd}'); n+=1
    if n==0: print('  (none)')
    print()

import re, glob
def strip_comments(s):
    out=[]; i,n=0,len(s); ins=False
    while i<n:
        c=s[i]
        if ins:
            out.append(c)
            if c=='"': ins=False
            i+=1
        elif c=='"': ins=True; out.append(c); i+=1
        elif c=='#':
            while i<n and s[i] not in '\r\n': out.append(' '); i+=1
        else: out.append(c); i+=1
    return ''.join(out)
def match_brace(s,p):
    d=0
    for i in range(p,len(s)):
        if s[i]=='{': d+=1
        elif s[i]=='}':
            d-=1
            if d==0: return i+1
    return None
idx={}
for f in sorted(glob.glob('characters/*.txt')):
    raw=open(f,encoding='windows-1252').read()
    st=strip_comments(raw)
    for m in re.finditer(r'(?m)^(\d+)\s*=\s*\{',st):
        cid=m.group(1)
        end=match_brace(st,st.index('{',m.start()))
        blk=st[m.start():end]
        def g(k):
            mm=re.search(r'(?<![A-Za-z_])'+k+r'\s*=\s*("[^"]*"|[^\s\}]+)',blk)
            return mm.group(1).strip('"') if mm else None
        idx[cid]=(f,g('name'),g('dynasty'),g('father'),g('mother'))
def show_where(label,**kw):
    print(f'### {label} ###')
    for cid,(f,nm,d,fa,mo) in sorted(idx.items(),key=lambda x:int(x[0])):
        if all({'dynasty':d,'father':fa,'mother':mo,'file':f}[k]==v for k,v in kw.items()):
            print(f'{cid:10} {f:28} name={nm} fa={fa} mo={mo}')
    print()
show_where('dynasty 234', dynasty='234')

import re, glob
def strip_comments(s):
    out=[]; i,n=0,len(s); ins=False
    while i<n:
        c=s[i]
        if ins:
            out.append(c)
            if c=='"': ins=False
            i+=1
        elif c=='"': ins=True; out.append(c); i+=1
        elif c=='#':
            while i<n and s[i] not in '\r\n': out.append(' '); i+=1
        else: out.append(c); i+=1
    return ''.join(out)
def match_brace(s,p):
    d=0
    for i in range(p,len(s)):
        if s[i]=='{': d+=1
        elif s[i]=='}':
            d-=1
            if d==0: return i+1
    return None
idx={}; rawblk={}
for f in sorted(glob.glob('characters/*.txt')):
    raw=open(f,encoding='windows-1252').read()
    st=strip_comments(raw)
    for m in re.finditer(r'(?m)^(\d+)\s*=\s*\{',st):
        cid=m.group(1)
        end=match_brace(st,st.index('{',m.start()))
        idx[cid]=(f,st[m.start():end])
        rawblk[cid]=raw[m.start():end]
def g(cid,k):
    mm=re.search(r'(?<![A-Za-z_])'+k+r'\s*=\s*("[^"]*"|[^\s\}]+)',idx[cid][1])
    return mm.group(1).strip('"') if mm else None
print('### ALL dangling employer refs ###')
n=0
for cid,(f,st) in sorted(idx.items(),key=lambda x:int(x[0])):
    for m in re.finditer(r'(?<![A-Za-z_])employer\s*=\s*(\d+)',st):
        t=m.group(1)
        if t not in idx:
            n+=1
            print(f'{n:2}. {f:32} char {cid:8} ({g(cid,"name")}) employer={t}')
print('total:',n)

def match_brace(s,p):
    d=0
    for i in range(p,len(s)):
        if s[i]=='{': d+=1
        elif s[i]=='}':
            d-=1
            if d==0: return i+1
    return None
def show(f,cid):
    raw=open(f,encoding='windows-1252').read()
    st=strip_comments(raw)
    m=re.search(r'(?m)^'+cid+r'\s*=\s*\{',st)
    print('='*80); print(f,'char',cid)
    print(raw[m.start():match_brace(st,st.index('{',m.start()))].replace('\r\n','\n') if m else 'MISSING')

import re
def strip_comments(s):
    out=[]; i,n=0,len(s); ins=False
    while i<n:
        c=s[i]
        if ins:
            out.append(c)
            if c=='"': ins=False
            i+=1
        elif c=='"': ins=True; out.append(c); i+=1
        elif c=='#':
            while i<n and s[i] not in '\r\n': out.append(' '); i+=1
        else: out.append(c); i+=1
    return ''.join(out)

        elif s[i]=='}':

    return None

import re, glob
def strip_comments(s):
    out=[]; i,n=0,len(s); ins=False
    while i<n:
        c=s[i]
        if ins:
            out.append(c)
            if c=='"': ins=False
            i+=1
        elif c=='"': ins=True; out.append(c); i+=1
        elif c=='#':
            while i<n and s[i] not in '\r\n': out.append(' '); i+=1
        else: out.append(c); i+=1
    return ''.join(out)
def match_brace(s,p):
    d=0
    for i in range(p,len(s)):
        if s[i]=='{': d+=1
        elif s[i]=='}':
            d-=1
            if d==0: return i+1
    return None
idx={}; dates={}
date_re=re.compile(r'(?m)^[ \t]*(\d{1,4}\.\d{1,2}\.\d{1,2})\s*=\s*\{')
for f in sorted(glob.glob('characters/*.txt')):
    raw=open(f,encoding='windows-1252').read()
    st=strip_comments(raw)
    for m in re.finditer(r'(?m)^(\d+)\s*=\s*\{',st):
        cid=m.group(1)
        end=match_brace(st,st.index('{',m.start()))
        blk=st[m.start():end]
        def g(k):
            mm=re.search(r'(?<![A-Za-z_])'+k+r'\s*=\s*("[^"]*"|[^\s\}]+)',blk)
            return mm.group(1).strip('"') if mm else None
        idx[cid]=(f,g('name'),g('dynasty'),g('father'),g('mother'))
        b=d=None
        for dm in date_re.finditer(blk):
            dk=dm.group(1)
            ob=blk.index('{',dm.start()); e2=match_brace(blk,ob)
            inner=blk[ob:e2]
            if re.search(r'(?<![A-Za-z_])birth\s*=',inner) and b is None: b=dk
            if re.search(r'(?<![A-Za-z_])death\s*=',inner): d=dk
        def yr(dk):
            return int(dk.split('.')[0]) if dk else None
        dates[cid]=(yr(b),yr(d))
def show_where(label,**kw):
    print(f'### {label} ###')
    for cid,(f,nm,d,fa,mo) in sorted(idx.items(),key=lambda x:int(x[0])):
        if all({'dynasty':d,'father':fa,'mother':mo,'file':f,'name':nm}[k]==v for k,v in kw.items()):
            print(f'{cid:10} {f:28} name={nm} fa={fa} mo={mo} dates={dates[cid]}')
    print()
print('### referrers to 212770 (Beatrice) ###')
for cid,(f,nm,d,fa,mo) in sorted(idx.items(),key=lambda x:int(x[0])):
    if fa=='212770' or mo=='212770': print(' child:',cid,f,nm,dates[cid])

import re
def strip_comments(s):
    out=[]; i,n=0,len(s); ins=False
    while i<n:
        c=s[i]
        if ins:
            out.append(c)
            if c=='"': ins=False
            i+=1
        elif c=='"': ins=True; out.append(c); i+=1
        elif c=='#':
            while i<n and s[i] not in '\r\n': out.append(' '); i+=1
        else: out.append(c); i+=1
    return ''.join(out)
def match_brace(s,p):
    d=0
    for i in range(p,len(s)):
        if s[i]=='{': d+=1
        elif s[i]=='}':
            d-=1
            if d==0: return i+1
    return None
def show(f,cid):
    raw=open(f,encoding='windows-1252').read()
    st=strip_comments(raw)
    m=re.search(r'(?m)^'+cid+r'\s*=\s*\{',st)
    print('='*80); print(f,'char',cid)
    print(raw[m.start():match_brace(st,st.index('{',m.start()))].replace('\r\n','\n') if m else 'MISSING')
show('characters/german.txt','30444')
show('characters/german.txt','260436')
show('characters/panjabi.txt','248073')
show('characters/panjabi.txt','248074')

import re, glob
# Date-sanity scan: parent/child chronology violations that suggest wrong IDs
def strip_comments(s):
    out=[]; i,n=0,len(s); ins=False
    while i<n:
        c=s[i]
        if ins:
            out.append(c)
            if c=='"': ins=False
            i+=1
        elif c=='"': ins=True; out.append(c); i+=1
        elif c=='#':
            while i<n and s[i] not in '\r\n': out.append(' '); i+=1
        else: out.append(c); i+=1
    return ''.join(out)
def match_brace(s,p):
    d=0
    for i in range(p,len(s)):
        if s[i]=='{': d+=1
        elif s[i]=='}':
            d-=1
            if d==0: return i+1
    return None
idx={}; bd={}
date_re=re.compile(r'(?m)^[ \t]*(\d{1,4}\.\d{1,2}\.\d{1,2})\s*=\s*\{')
for f in sorted(glob.glob('characters/*.txt')):
    raw=open(f,encoding='windows-1252').read()
    st=strip_comments(raw)
    for m in re.finditer(r'(?m)^(\d+)\s*=\s*\{',st):
        cid=m.group(1)
        end=match_brace(st,st.index('{',m.start()))
        blk=st[m.start():end]
        def g(k):
            mm=re.search(r'(?<![A-Za-z_])'+k+r'\s*=\s*("[^"]*"|[^\s\}]+)',blk)
            return mm.group(1).strip('"') if mm else None
        idx[cid]=(f,g('name'),g('father'),g('mother'),g('female'))
        b=d=None
        for dm in date_re.finditer(blk):
            ob=blk.index('{',dm.start()); e2=match_brace(blk,ob)
            inner=blk[ob:e2]
            if re.search(r'(?<![A-Za-z_])birth\s*=',inner) and b is None: b=dm.group(1)
            if re.search(r'(?<![A-Za-z_])death\s*=',inner): d=dm.group(1)
        bd[cid]=(b,d)
def Y(dk):
    return int(dk.split('.')[0]) if dk else None
print('### father-link violations (child born before father born, or >70y after father death) ###')
n=0
for cid,(f,nm,fa,mo,fem) in sorted(idx.items(),key=lambda x:int(x[0])):
    if not fa or fa not in idx: continue
    cb=Y(bd[cid][0]); pb=Y(bd[fa][0]); pd=Y(bd[fa][1])
    bad=None
    if cb is not None and pb is not None and cb<pb: bad=f'child b.{cb} BEFORE father b.{pb}'
    if cb is not None and pd is not None and cb-pd>70: bad=f'child b.{cb}, father d.{pd} (+{cb-pd}y)'
    if bad:
        n+=1
        print(f'{f:30} child {cid} ({nm} b.{cb}) father {fa} ({idx[fa][1]} b.{pb} d.{pd}): {bad}')
print('count:',n)
print()
print('### mother-link violations (child born before mother born, or >3y after mother death) ###')
n=0
for cid,(f,nm,fa,mo,fem) in sorted(idx.items(),key=lambda x:int(x[0])):
    if not mo or mo not in idx: continue
    cb=Y(bd[cid][0]); pb=Y(bd[mo][0]); pd=Y(bd[mo][1])
    bad=None
    if cb is not None and pb is not None and cb<pb: bad=f'child b.{cb} BEFORE mother b.{pb}'
    if cb is not None and pd is not None and cb-pd>3: bad=f'child b.{cb}, mother d.{pd} (+{cb-pd}y)'
    if bad:
        n+=1
        print(f'{f:30} child {cid} ({nm} b.{cb}) mother {mo} ({idx[mo][1]} b.{pb} d.{pd}): {bad}')
print('count:',n)
print()
print('### fathers flagged female / mothers flagged male? (spot check: father with female=yes) ###')
n=0
for cid,(f,nm,fa,mo,fem) in idx.items():
    if fa and fa in idx and idx[fa][4]=='yes':

        pass
print('female-fathers:',n)

import re

def edit(path, pairs):
    raw = open(path, encoding='windows-1252').read()
    for old, new, expect in pairs:
        n = raw.count(old)
        assert n == expect, f'{path}: {old!r} found {n}x, expected {expect}x'
        raw = raw.replace(old, new)
    open(path, 'w', encoding='windows-1252', newline='').write(raw)
    print(f'{path}: OK ({len(pairs)} edits)')

# 1. Bedouin fictional patriarchs: chain by generation (date-consistent)
edit('characters/bedouin_arabic.txt', [
    ('\tfather=291001\n', '\tfather=251000\n', 1),  # 251001 Muhammad b.774 -> Ali b.740
    ('\tfather=291002\n', '\tfather=251001\n', 1),  # 251002 Hussayn b.831 -> Muhammad b.774
    ('\tfather=291004\n', '\tfather=251003\n', 1),  # 251004 Al-Muhtadi b.770 -> Kadi b.732
    ('\tfather=291005\n', '\tfather=251004\n', 1),  # 251005 Abdullah b.840 -> Al-Muhtadi b.770
])
# 2. Breton off-by-one: Alfred 91245 (b.1015, same dynasty) is father of 10060 (b.1056)
edit('characters/breton.txt', [

])

edit('characters/frankish.txt', [

])

python3 - <<'PY'
import glob, re
def strip_comments(s):
    out=[]; i,n=0,len(s); ins=False
    while i<n:
        c=s[i]
        if ins:
            out.append(c)
            if c=='"': ins=False
            i+=1
        elif c=='"': ins=True; out.append(c); i+=1
        elif c=='#':
            while i<n and s[i] not in '\r\n': out.append(' '); i+=1
        else: out.append(c); i+=1
    return ''.join(out)
def match_brace(s,p):
    d=0
    for i in range(p,len(s)):
        if s[i]=='{': d+=1
        elif s[i]=='}':
            d-=1
            if d==0: return i+1
    return None
blocks={}; dups=[]; unb=0
for f in sorted(glob.glob('characters/*.txt')):
    raw=open(f,encoding='windows-1252').read()
    st=strip_comments(raw)
    for m in re.finditer(r'(?m)^(\d+)\s*=\s*\{',st):
        pos,cid=m.start(),m.group(1)
        end=match_brace(st,st.index('{',pos))
        if end is None: unb+=1; print('UNBALANCED',f,cid); continue
        if cid in blocks: dups.append(cid)
        else: blocks[cid]=(f,st[pos:end])
print('blocks:',len(blocks),'dups:',dups,'unbalanced:',unb)
for k in ['father','mother','add_spouse','remove_spouse','employer','killer','add_friend','add_rival','add_lover','add_consort','remove_friend']:
    tot=0; dang=[]
    for cid,(f,st) in blocks.items():
        for m in re.finditer(r'(?<![A-Za-z_])'+k+r'\s*=\s*(\d+)',st):
            tot+=1
            if m.group(1) not in blocks: dang.append((f,cid,m.group(1)))
    extra = f' values={sorted(set(d[2] for d in dang))}' if dang else ''
    print(f'{k:15} total={tot:>6} dangling={len(dang):>3}{extra}')
# confirm fixed links resolve + date check on fixed ones
date_re=re.compile(r'(?m)^[ \t]*(\d{1,4}\.\d{1,2}\.\d{1,2})\s*=\s*\{')
def bd(cid):
    b=d=None
    for dm in date_re.finditer(blocks[cid][1]):
        ob=blocks[cid][1].index('{',dm.start()); e2=match_brace(blocks[cid][1],ob)
        inner=blocks[cid][1][ob:e2]
        if re.search(r'(?<![A-Za-z_])birth\s*=',inner) and b is None: b=dm.group(1)
        if re.search(r'(?<![A-Za-z_])death\s*=',inner): d=dm.group(1)
    return b,d
print()
for child,par in [('251001','251000'),('251002','251001'),('251004','251003'),('251005','251004'),
                  ('10060','91245'),('251184','251193'),('260495','260494'),
                  ('248206','248204'),('248207','248205'),('248208','248205'),('247098','247095')]:
    print(f'child {child} {bd(child)} <- parent {par} {bd(par)}')
print('247095 in bodpa:', '247095' in blocks, blocks.get('247095',('?',''))[0])

# Character ID audit & fixes

Full audit of all `characters/*.txt` (37,700 blocks after fixes): every `father=`, `mother=`,
`add_spouse=`, `remove_spouse=`, `employer=`, `killer=`, `add/respectively remove_friend/rival/lover/consort`
reference was checked against all defined character IDs, plus a parent/child date-sanity scan.

Result before fixes: **0 duplicate IDs**, **11 dangling `father=`**, **1 dangling `mother=`**,
**2 genuinely dangling `employer=`**, and **2 absurd-but-resolving father links** (sons of a
3rd-century prophet). All fixed below. No other changes were made to history data.

## Fixes applied

| # | File | Character | Change | Why |
|---|------|-----------|--------|-----|
| 1 | `bedouin_arabic.txt` | 251001 Muhammad (b.774) | `father=291001` → `251000` | 291001 never existed. 251000 Ali (b.740, d.780, same dynasty 1060000, previously childless) is the patriarch. |
| 2 | `bedouin_arabic.txt` | 251002 Hussayn (b.831) | `father=291002` → `251001` | 291002 never existed. Chained to 251001 (d.836, age 57 — date-consistent; the patriarch died 51y before this birth). |

| 5 | `breton.txt` | 10060 Guimarc'h (b.1056) | `father=91244` → `91245` | Off-by-one typo; 91244 doesn't exist. 91245 Alfred (b.1015, same dynasty 20017, age 41 at birth) fits exactly. |

Reconstructed as lowborn (no `dynasty=` line), bon, bodpa; name/dates marked as placeholders in comments. |

| 16 | `panjabi.txt` | 248075 Ibrahim Shah | `1246.1.1 = { employer = 1060080 }` commented out | 1060080 doesn't exist (looks like a dynasty ID pasted into a character field); no identifiable employer. Kept as `#` comment. |

## Deliberately left alone

- **`employer = 0` (36 cases)** — a consistent idiom across files (military-order brothers, El Cid, Delhi sultans…), meaning "no employer". Not an error.
- **6 mother-date gaps of 6–10 years** (e.g. bodpa 247124/247125/247126 vs mother d.798; german 7658; nepali 12374; irish 166103) — the mother IDs look correct and the gaps are typical year-approximation noise. Fixing those would mean rewriting history dates, which is out of scope for an ID audit.
- **`248209`'s `#father=248203`** — already commented out by the author; left as is.
- No duplicate IDs were found anywhere (37,700 unique).

## Verification (re-ran after edits)

- 0 duplicate IDs, 0 unbalanced blocks, 0 dangling `father` / `mother` / spouse / killer / friend / rival / lover / consort refs.
- Remaining dangling `employer` refs: only the 36 intentional `employer = 0`.
- Every re-pointed parent/child pair is chronologically consistent (see table dates above).
- Byte-safe edits: files kept their original Windows-1252 encoding and LF line endings.

python3 - <<'PY'
import glob, re
def strip_comments(s):
    out=[]; i,n=0,len(s); ins=False
    while i<n:
        c=s[i]
        if ins:
            out.append(c)
            if c=='"': ins=False
            i+=1
        elif c=='"': ins=True; out.append(c); i+=1
        elif c=='#':
            while i<n and s[i] not in '\r\n': out.append(' '); i+=1
        else: out.append(c); i+=1
    return ''.join(out)

all_ids=set()
print(f"{'file':32} {'n':>5} {'min':>9} {'max':>9} {'span':>9} {'in-order%':>9} {'max-gap':>9}")
print('-'*100)
tot_span=0
for f in sorted(glob.glob('characters/*.txt')):
    raw=open(f,encoding='windows-1252').read()
    st=strip_comments(raw)
    ids=[int(m.group(1)) for m in re.finditer(r'(?m)^(\d+)\s*=\s*\{',st)]
    all_ids.update(ids)
    if not ids: continue
    srt=sorted(ids)
    span=max(ids)-min(ids)+1
    inorder=sum(1 for a,b in zip(ids,ids[1:]) if b>a)
    pct=100.0*inorder/max(1,len(ids)-1)
    gaps=[b-a for a,b in zip(srt,srt[1:])]
    maxgap=max(gaps)-1 if gaps else 0
    print(f"{f:32} {len(ids):>5} {min(ids):>9} {max(ids):>9} {span:>9} {pct:>8.1f}% {maxgap:>9,}")
    tot_span+=span
print('-'*100)
print('unique IDs total:',len(all_ids),'| global min:',min(all_ids),'max:',max(all_ids))
print('global span:',max(all_ids)-min(all_ids)+1,'| density: %.2f%%' % (100*len(all_ids)/(max(all_ids)-min(all_ids)+1)))

def strip_comments(s):
    out=[]; i,n=0,len(s); ins=False
    while i<n:
        c=s[i]
        if ins:

import re
def strip_comments(s):
    out=[]; i,n=0,len(s); ins=False
    while i<n:
        c=s[i]
        if ins:
            out.append(c)
            if c=='"': ins=False
            i+=1
        elif c=='"': ins=True; out.append(c); i+=1
        elif c=='#':
            while i<n and s[i] not in '\r\n': out.append(' '); i+=1
        else: out.append(c); i+=1
    return ''.join(out)
def match_brace(s,p):
    d=0
    for i in range(p,len(s)):
        if s[i]=='{': d+=1
        elif s[i]=='}':
            d-=1
            if d==0: return i+1
    return None

def edit(path, pairs):
    raw = open(path, encoding='windows-1252').read()
    for old, new, expect in pairs:
        n = raw.count(old)
        assert n == expect, f'{path}: {old!r} found {n}x, expected {expect}x'
        raw = raw.replace(old, new)
    open(path, 'w', encoding='windows-1252', newline='').write(raw)
    print(f'{path}: OK ({len(pairs)} edits)')

edit('characters/bedouin_arabic.txt', [

import glob, re, os
SRC='characters'; OUT='character_sheets'
os.makedirs(OUT, exist_ok=True)
def strip_comments(s):
    out=[]; i,n=0,len(s); ins=False
    while i<n:
        c=s[i]
        if ins:
            out.append(c)
            if c=='"': ins=False
            i+=1
        elif c=='"': ins=True; out.append(c); i+=1
        elif c=='#':
            while i<n and s[i] not in '\r\n': out.append(' '); i+=1
        else: out.append(c); i+=1
    return ''.join(out)
def match_brace(s,p):
    d=0
    for i in range(p,len(s)):
        if s[i]=='{': d+=1
        elif s[i]=='}':
            d-=1
            if d==0: return i+1
    return None
def clean_val(v):
    v=v.strip()
    if len(v)>=2 and v.startswith('"') and v.endswith('"'): return v[1:-1]
    return v
def norm(s): return s.replace('\r\n','\n').replace('\r','\n')
def tok_re(k): return re.compile(r'(?<![A-Za-z_])'+k+r'\s*=\s*("[^"]*"|[^\s\}]+)')
def get_tok(b,k):
    m=tok_re(k).search(b)
    return m.group(1).strip() if m else None
files=sorted(glob.glob(f'{SRC}/*.txt'))
all_blocks={}; dups=[]
for f in files:
    raw=open(f,encoding='windows-1252').read()
    st=strip_comments(raw)
    for m in re.finditer(r'(?m)^(\d+)\s*=\s*\{',st):
        pos,cid=m.start(),m.group(1)
        end=match_brace(st,st.index('{',pos))
        if end is None: print('UNBALANCED',f,cid); continue
        rec={'file':f,'raw':raw[pos:end],'st':st[pos:end],'line':raw.count('\n',0,pos)+1}
        if cid in all_blocks: dups.append(cid)
        else: all_blocks[cid]=rec
print('blocks:',len(all_blocks),'dups:',dups)
date_re=re.compile(r'(?m)^[ \t]*(\d{1,4}\.\d{1,2}\.\d{1,2})\s*=\s*\{')
def date_spans(b):
    r=[]
    for m in date_re.finditer(b):
        ob=b.index('{',m.start()); e=match_brace(b,ob)
        if e: r.append((m.group(1),m.start(),e))
    return r
def mask_spans(s,sp):
    b=list(s)
    for _,a,e in sp:
        for i in range(a,e):
            if b[i] not in '\r\n': b[i]=' '
    return ''.join(b)
name_index={}
for cid,r in all_blocks.items():
    t=get_tok(r['st'],'name')
    name_index[cid]=clean_val(t) if t else '(no name= line)'
children={}
for cid,r in all_blocks.items():
    for m in re.finditer(r'(?<![A-Za-z_])(father|mother)\s*=\s*(\d+)',r['st']):
        children.setdefault(m.group(2),[]).append((cid,m.group(1)))
def first_birth_death(cid):
    r=all_blocks.get(cid)
    if not r: return None,None
    b=d=None
    for dt,a,e in date_spans(r['st']):
        inner=r['st'][a:e]
        if b is None:
            m=tok_re('birth').search(inner)
            if m:
                v=clean_val(m.group(1)); b=dt if v.lower()=='yes' else v
        if d is None:
            m=re.search(r'(?<![A-Za-z_])death\s*=\s*(\{[^{}]*\}|"[^"]*"|[^\s\}]+)',inner,re.S)
            if m:
                dv=m.group(1)
                if dv.startswith('{'): dv=re.sub(r'\s+',' ',dv).strip()
                else:
                    dv=clean_val(dv)
                    if dv.lower()=='yes': dv=dt
                d=dv
        if b and d: break
    return b,d
def person_ref(pid):
    if pid in all_blocks: return f'`{pid}` — {name_index[pid]} (`{all_blocks[pid]["file"]}`)'
    return f'`{pid}` — (ID not found in these history files)'
summ_re=re.compile(r'(?<![A-Za-z_])(birth|death|trait|add_trait|remove_trait|give_nickname|add_spouse|remove_spouse|add_claim|create_bloodline|prestige|piety|religion|culture|dynasty|employer|name|give_job_title|set_government_type)\s*=\s*(\{[^{}]*\}|"[^"]*"|[^\s\}]+)',re.S)
def summarize(inner):
    p=[]
    for m in summ_re.finditer(inner):
        p.append(f'{m.group(1)}={re.sub(chr(92)+chr(115)+"+"," ",m.group(2)).strip()}')
    return '; '.join(p) if p else '(see verbatim block below)'
KNOWN={'name','female','dynasty','religion','culture','martial','diplomacy','intrigue','stewardship','learning','dna','properties','father','mother','trait','add_trait'}
blood=[(c,r) for c,r in all_blocks.items() if 'create_bloodline' in r['st']]
blood.sort(key=lambda x:(x[1]['file'],int(x[0])))
print('bloodline holders:',len(blood))
def md_cell(s): return str(s).replace('|','\\|').replace('\n',' ')
sheets=[]
for cid,r in blood:
    bs,br=r['st'],r['raw']
    spans=date_spans(bs); base=mask_spans(bs,spans)
    nt=get_tok(base,'name'); name=clean_val(nt) if nt else '(no name= line)'
    ft=get_tok(base,'female'); dt=get_tok(base,'dynasty'); rt=get_tok(base,'religion'); ct=get_tok(base,'culture')
    fat=get_tok(base,'father'); mot=get_tok(base,'mother'); dna=get_tok(base,'dna'); prp=get_tok(base,'properties')
    attrs={k:get_tok(base,k) for k in ('martial','diplomacy','intrigue','stewardship','learning')}
    traits=[m.group(1).strip() for m in re.finditer(r'(?<![A-Za-z_])(?:add_trait|trait)\s*=\s*("[^"]*"|[^\s\}]+)',base)]
    blines=[]
    for d,a,e in spans:
        inner=bs[a:e]
        for m in re.finditer(r'(?<![A-Za-z_])create_bloodline\s*=\s*\{',inner):
            ob=inner.index('{',m.start()); en=match_brace(inner,ob)
            tm=re.search(r'(?<![A-Za-z_])type\s*=\s*([A-Za-z0-9_]+)',inner[ob:en])
            bt=tm.group(1) if tm else '(no type= found)'
            ls=br.rfind('\n',0,a+m.start())+1
            blines.append((d,bt,norm(br[ls:a+en])))
    if not blines:
        for m in re.finditer(r'(?<![A-Za-z_])create_bloodline\s*=\s*\{',base):
            ob=base.index('{',m.start()); en=match_brace(base,ob)
            tm=re.search(r'(?<![A-Za-z_])type\s*=\s*([A-Za-z0-9_]+)',base[ob:en])
            bt=tm.group(1) if tm else '(no type= found)'
            ls=br.rfind('\n',0,m.start())+1
            blines.append(('(top level, no date)',bt,norm(br[ls:en])))
    slug='_'.join(re.sub(r'\W+','',t) for _,t,_ in blines) or 'bloodline'
    fname=f'{cid}_{slug}.md'
    spouses=[]
    for d,a,e in spans:
        for m in re.finditer(r'(?<![A-Za-z_])(add_spouse|remove_spouse)\s*=\s*(\d+)',bs[a:e]):
            spouses.append((d,m.group(1),m.group(2)))
    kids=[]
    for kcid,role in sorted(children.get(cid,[]),key=lambda x:int(x[0])):
        kb,_=first_birth_death(kcid); kids.append((kcid,role,kb))
    chrono=[(d,summarize(bs[a:e])) for d,a,e in spans]
    verb=[(d,norm(br[a:e]).rstrip()) for d,a,e in spans]
    birth,death=first_birth_death(cid)
    extras=sorted(k for k in set(re.findall(r'(?m)^[ \t]*([A-Za-z_]+)\s*=',base)) if k not in KNOWN and k!='')
    L=[f'# {name} — character {cid}','','> Transcribed as-is from the history files. Nothing invented; values below are the exact tokens from the source block.',f'> Source: `{r["file"]}` (line {r["line"]}), block `{cid} = {{ ... }}`.','> Display only: line endings normalized (CRLF → LF), encoding converted (Windows-1252 → UTF-8). Inline `#` comments are preserved in the verbatim sections.','','## Identity (as in file)','','| Field | Value in file |','|---|---|',f'| Character ID | `{cid}` |']
    L.append(f'| Name | `{md_cell(nt)}` |' if nt else '| Name | — (absent) |')
    L.append('| Sex | female (`female = yes`) |' if ft and ft.lower()=='yes' else (f'| Sex | `{md_cell(ft)}` (unusual `female=` value — see verbatim) |' if ft else '| Sex | male (default — no `female=` line) |'))
    L.append(f'| Dynasty | `{md_cell(dt)}` |' if dt else '| Dynasty | — (absent; lowborn / no dynasty line) |')
    L.append(f'| Religion | `{md_cell(rt)}` |' if rt else '| Religion | — (absent) |')
    L.append(f'| Culture | `{md_cell(ct)}` |' if ct else '| Culture | — (absent) |')
    L.append(f'| Father | {person_ref(fat)} |' if fat else '| Father | — (no `father=` line) |')
    L.append(f'| Mother | {person_ref(mot)} |' if mot else '| Mother | — (no `mother=` line) |')
    L.append(f'| DNA | `{md_cell(dna)}` |' if dna else '| DNA | — (absent) |')
    L.append(f'| Properties | `{md_cell(prp)}` |' if prp else '| Properties | — (absent) |')
    L.append(''); L.append('## Attributes (as in file)'); L.append(''); L.append('| martial | diplomacy | intrigue | stewardship | learning |'); L.append('|---|---|---|---|---|')
    L.append('| '+' | '.join(f'`{attrs[k]}`' if attrs[k] else '—' for k in ('martial','diplomacy','intrigue','stewardship','learning'))+' |')
    L.append(''); L.append('## Traits (base entries, as in file)'); L.append('')
    if traits:
        for t in traits: L.append(f'- `{md_cell(t)}`')
    else: L.append('_None at top level._')
    L.append('')
    if extras:
        L.append('## Other top-level fields (as in file)'); L.append('')
        for k in extras:
            for m in re.finditer(r'(?m)^[ \t]*'+re.escape(k)+r'\s*=\s*("[^"]*"|[^\s\}]+|\{)',base):
                v=m.group(1)
                L.append(f'- `{k} = {{ ... }}` (see verbatim block)' if v.startswith('{') else f'- `{k} = {v}`')
        L.append('')
    L.append('## Bloodline(s) (as in file)'); L.append('')
    for d,t,snip in blines:
        L.append(f'### `{t}` — granted {d}'); L.append(''); L.append('```text'); L.append(snip); L.append('```'); L.append('')
    L.append('## Family & relations (IDs as in file; names looked up from the same history files)'); L.append('')
    L.append(f'- Father: {person_ref(fat)}' if fat else '- Father: — (no `father=` line)')
    L.append(f'- Mother: {person_ref(mot)}' if mot else '- Mother: — (no `mother=` line)')
    if spouses:
        L.append('- Spouses:')
        for d,act,pid in spouses: L.append(f'  - {"added" if act=="add_spouse" else "removed"} {d}: {person_ref(pid)}')
    else: L.append('- Spouses: — (no `add_spouse=` / `remove_spouse=` lines)')
    if kids:
        L.append(f'- Recorded children ({len(kids)} other block(s) with `father={cid}` or `mother={cid}`):')
        for kcid,role,kb in kids: L.append(f'  - `{role}={cid}`: {person_ref(kcid)}'+(f', born {kb}' if kb else ''))
    else: L.append(f'- Recorded children: — (no block has `father={cid}` or `mother={cid}`)')
    L.append(''); L.append('## Chronology (date order as in file; statements verbatim)'); L.append('')
    if chrono:
        for d,s in chrono: L.append(f'- **{d}** — `{md_cell(s)}`')
    else: L.append('_No dated history entries._')
    L.append(''); L.append('## History entries (verbatim)'); L.append('')
    if verb:
        for d,rv in verb: L.append(f'### {d}'); L.append(''); L.append('```text'); L.append(rv); L.append('```'); L.append('')
    else: L.append('_No dated history entries._'); L.append('')
    L.append('## Full character block (verbatim)'); L.append(''); L.append('```text'); L.append(norm(br).rstrip()); L.append('```'); L.append('')
    open(os.path.join(OUT,fname),'w',encoding='utf-8',newline='\n').write('\n'.join(L))
    sheets.append({'file':fname,'cid':cid,'name':name,'src':r['file'],'types':', '.join(t for _,t,_ in blines),'birth':birth or '—','death':death or '—'})
idx=['# Character sheets — bloodline holders','',f'{len(sheets)} bloodline holders transcribed as-is from `characters/*.txt` (every block containing `create_bloodline`). No values invented.','','Notes:','- Each sheet quotes its source block verbatim, plus field-by-field tables of the exact tokens in the file.','- Display only: CRLF → LF line endings, Windows-1252 → UTF-8. Inline `#` comments appear in the verbatim sections.','- Dynasty names are NOT in this repo (only numeric `dynasty=` IDs), so sheets list the ID without inventing a name.','- Father / mother / spouse / children names are looked up from these same history files by character ID and labelled as such.',"- Where the file says `birth=yes` / `death=yes`, the Born/Died columns show that entry's date key (also from the file); the verbatim sections keep the raw `yes`.",'', '| # | Character | ID | Source file | Bloodline | Born | Died |','|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|']
for i,s in enumerate(sheets,1):
    idx.append(f'| {i} | [{md_cell(s["name"])}](./{s["file"]}) | `{s["cid"]}` | `{s["src"]}` | `{md_cell(s["types"])}` | {md_cell(s["birth"])} | {md_cell(s["death"])} |')
idx.append('')
open(os.path.join(OUT,'README.md'),'w',encoding='utf-8',newline='\n').write('\n'.join(idx))
print('wrote',len(sheets),'sheets + README')

# Character ID audit & fixes

Full audit of all `characters/*.txt` (37,700 blocks after fixes): every `father=`, `mother=`,
`add_spouse=`, `remove_spouse=`, `employer=`, `killer=`, `add/respectively remove_friend/rival/lover/consort`
reference was checked against all defined character IDs, plus a parent/child date-sanity scan.

Result before fixes: **0 duplicate IDs**, **11 dangling `father=`**, **1 dangling `mother=`**,
**2 genuinely dangling `employer=`**, **2 absurd-but-resolving father links** (sons of a
3rd-century prophet), and **1 typo'd block ID**. All fixed below. No other changes were made to history data.

## Fixes applied

| # | File | Character | Change | Why |
|---|------|-----------|--------|-----|
| 1 | `bedouin_arabic.txt` | 251001 Muhammad (b.774) | `father=291001` → `251000` | 291001 never existed. 251000 Ali (b.740, d.780, same dynasty 1060000, previously childless) is the patriarch. |
| 2 | `bedouin_arabic.txt` | 251002 Hussayn (b.831) | `father=291002` → `251001` | 291002 never existed. Chained to 251001 (d.836, age 57 — date-consistent; the patriarch died 51y before this birth). |
| 3 | `bedouin_arabic.txt` | 251004 Al-Muhtadi (b.770) | `father=291004` → `251003` | 291004 never existed. 251003 Kadi (b.732, d.775, same dynasty 1060002, previously childless) is the patriarch. |
| 4 | `bedouin_arabic.txt` | 251005 Abdullah (b.840) | `father=291005` → `251004` | 291005 never existed. Chained to 251004 (d.845 — date-consistent; the patriarch died 65y before this birth). |
| 5 | `breton.txt` | 10060 Guimarc'h (b.1056) | `father=91244` → `91245` | Off-by-one typo; 91244 doesn't exist. 91245 Alfred (b.1015, same dynasty 20017, age 41 at birth) fits exactly. |
| 6 | `frankish.txt` | 212770 Beatrice (b.1125) | `father=213030` removed (kept as `#` comment) | 213030 doesn't exist; block is orphaned (no dynasty, no referrers) and no candidate fits. |
| 7 | `hausa.txt` | 251184 Kacinna (b.1066, "princess of Daura") | `father=1228658` → `251193` | 1228658 doesn't exist. 251193 Atuma (b.1043, Daura king, age 23 at her birth) is the only right-generation candidate. |
| 8 | `irish.txt` | 260495 Donnchad (b.1248) | `father=250494` → `260494` | Digit typo (`250494` → `260494`); 250494 doesn't exist. 260494 Domnall (b.1215, same dynasty, age 33 at birth) fits exactly. |
| 9 | `kanuri.txt` | 248748 Hummay (b.1050) | `father=1000228558` removed (kept as `#` comment) | Garbage 10-digit ID. Hummay ("first muslim ruler") heads a clean father→son chain, so he is the founder. |
| 10 | `maghreb_arabic.txt` | 273088 'Abu-Umar (b.1060) | `father=1144550` removed (kept as `#` comment) | 1144550 doesn't exist; sole member of dynasty 1069081 with no candidate father. |
| 11 | `mongol.txt` | 248206 Chotan (b.1227, female) | `father=248200` → `248204` | 248200 doesn't exist. 248204 Yasaur (b.1200, dynasty patriarch, age 27 at birth) is the only candidate. |
| 12 | `mongol.txt` | 248207 Altan (b.1261, sunni) | `father=248201` → `248205` | 248201 is **Mani, the 3rd-century prophet** (b.216!) — obvious typo. 248205 Bolkhada (b.1225, converted sunni 1260, age 36 at birth) matches on age, dynasty, and religion. The author's own sibling block 248210 already uses `father=248205`. |
| 13 | `mongol.txt` | 248208 Doguz (b.1264, sunni, female) | `father=248201` → `248205` | Same Mani typo as #12; same evidence (Bolkhada age 39 at birth). |
| 14 | `bodpa.txt` | **created 247095** (consort, b.672, d.745) | new block between 247094 and 247096 | 247095 was referenced twice — `247091`'s 690.1.1 `add_consort=c_247095` and `247098`'s `mother=247095` (`child_of_consort`) — but never defined. Reconstructed as lowborn (no `dynasty=` line), bon, bodpa; name/dates marked as placeholders in comments. |
| 15 | `german.txt` | 170341 Johannes | `employer=330444` → `30444` | Extra-digit typo. 30444 is Volkwin of the Livonian Swordbrothers, dynasty von Naumburg (matches the inline comment), died Sep 1236 — the employment starts Jan 1236. |
| 16 | `panjabi.txt` | 248075 Ibrahim Shah | `1246.1.1 = { employer = 1060080 }` commented out | 1060080 doesn't exist (looks like a dynasty ID pasted into a character field); no identifiable employer. Kept as `#` comment. |
| 17 | `kurdish.txt` | Hasanwayh (b.925, dynasty 1062496) | block ID `2601031` → `261031` | Extra-`0` typo: sits right after 261030 Husayn (same dynasty) as the file's last block. 261031 was free and nothing referenced either ID, so a pure rename. |

## Deliberately left alone

- **`employer = 0` (36 cases)** — a consistent idiom across files (military-order brothers, El Cid, Delhi sultans…), meaning "no employer". Not an error.
- **6 mother-date gaps of 6–10 years** (e.g. bodpa 247124/247125/247126 vs mother d.798; german 7658; nepali 12374; irish 166103) — the mother IDs look correct and the gaps are typical year-approximation noise. Fixing those would mean rewriting history dates, which is out of scope for an ID audit.
- **`248209`'s `#father=248203`** — already commented out by the author; left as is.
- No duplicate IDs were found anywhere (37,700 unique).

## Verification (re-ran after edits)

- 0 duplicate IDs, 0 unbalanced blocks, 0 dangling `father` / `mother` / spouse / killer / friend / rival / lover / consort refs.
- Remaining dangling `employer` refs: only the 36 intentional `employer = 0`.
- Every re-pointed parent/child pair is chronologically consistent (see table dates above).
- Byte-safe edits: files kept their original Windows-1252 encoding and LF line endings.
