python3 - <<'PY'
import glob, re
def strip_comments(s):
    out=[]; i,n=0,len(s); ins=False
    while i<n:
        c=s[i]
        if ins:
            out.append(c)
            if c=='"': ins=False
            i+=1
        elif c=='"': ins=True; out.append(c); i+=1
        elif c=='#':
            while i<n and s[i] not in '\r\n': out.append(' '); i+=1
        else: out.append(c); i+=1
    return ''.join(out)

perfile={}
all_ids=[]
for f in sorted(glob.glob('characters/*.txt')):
    st=strip_comments(open(f,encoding='windows-1252').read())
    ids=[int(m.group(1)) for m in re.finditer(r'(?m)^(\d+)\s*=\s*\{',st)]
    perfile[f]=ids
    all_ids.extend(ids)

L=[]
L.append('# Character ID ordering & gap analysis')
L.append('')
L.append('Read-only analysis of block order and ID gaps across `characters/*.txt` (37,700 blocks).')
L.append('No history files were changed for this report. Method: blocks parsed in file order via `^ID = {`;')
L.append('comment-aware brace matching; IDs verified unique (see `ID_FIXES.md`).')
L.append('')
L.append('## TL;DR')
L.append('')
L.append('- Files are **organized in ascending runs (families/ranges appended in chunks)**, not globally sorted: 82–100% of adjacent ID pairs are ascending, but each file contains several out-of-order chunks.')
L.append('- Gaps are **huge and harmless**: 37,700 IDs spread over a 0–2.6M space (1.45% dense). The engine only needs uniqueness.')
L.append('- The `1059xxx–10599xx` cluster and dense `248xxx` block are **deliberate content ranges**, not accidents.')
L.append('- One gap-causing typo was already fixed: kurdish `2601031` → `261031` (see `ID_FIXES.md` #17).')
L.append('- Recommendation: leave order and gaps as-is (ID stability matters for saves/tools; sorting would scatter family groups).')
L.append('')
L.append('## Global picture')
L.append('')
L.append(f'- Unique IDs: **{len(set(all_ids)):,}** | global min **{min(all_ids)}** | global max **{max(all_ids):,}**')
span=max(all_ids)-min(all_ids)+1
L.append(f'- Global span: **{span:,}** → density **{100*len(set(all_ids))/span:.2f}%**')
L.append('')
# histogram buckets of 100k
from collections import Counter
b=Counter()
for i in all_ids: b[i//100000]+=1
L.append('### Blocks per 100k ID bucket (global)')
L.append('')
L.append('| Bucket | Blocks | Bar |')
L.append('|---|---|---|')
mx=max(b.values())
for k in range(0, max(all_ids)//100000+1):
    n=b.get(k,0)
    bar='█'*(1+int(30*n/mx)) if n else '·'
    L.append(f'| {k*100}-{k*100+99}k | {n:>5,} | {bar} |')
L.append('')
L.append('## Per-file ordering stats')
L.append('')
L.append('`runs` = number of ascending chunks (1 = perfectly sorted file). `displaced` = blocks appearing after a higher ID.')
L.append('')
L.append('| File | Blocks | Min | Max | Runs | Adjacent-ordered | Displaced | Max gap |')
L.append('|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|')
tot_runs=0
for f,ids in perfile.items():
    s=sorted(ids)
    inorder=sum(1 for a,x in zip(ids,ids[1:]) if x>a)
    pct=100.0*inorder/max(1,len(ids)-1) if len(ids)>1 else 100.0
    mx=-1; disp=0; runs=1 if ids else 0
    for i in ids:
        if i<mx: disp+=1; runs+=1
        else: mx=i
    gaps=[y-x-1 for x,y in zip(s,s[1:])]
    mg=max(gaps) if gaps else 0
    tot_runs+=runs
    L.append(f'| `{f}` | {len(ids):,} | {min(ids):,} | {max(ids):,} | {runs} | {pct:.1f}% | {disp:,} | {mg:,} |')
L.append('')
L.append(f'Total ascending runs across all files: **{tot_runs}** in 117 files.')
L.append('')
L.append('## Largest gaps (top 25, per file)')
L.append('')
L.append('| # | File | Gap size | Between IDs |')
L.append('|---|---|---|---|')
gaps=[]
for f,ids in perfile.items():
    s=sorted(ids)
    for x,y in zip(s,s[1:]):
        if y-x-1>0: gaps.append((y-x-1,f,x,y))
gaps.sort(reverse=True)
for i,(g,f,x,y) in enumerate(gaps[:25],1):
    L.append(f'| {i} | `{f}` | {g:,} | {x:,} → {y:,} |')
L.append('')
L.append('## Example: chunk layout of `characters/english.txt`')
L.append('')
ids=perfile['characters/english.txt']
runs=[]; cur=[ids[0]]
for i in ids[1:]:
    if i<cur[-1]: runs.append(cur); cur=[i]
    else: cur.append(i)
runs.append(cur)
L.append(f'{len(ids)} blocks in {len(runs)} ascending chunks (file order):')
L.append('')
L.append('| Chunk | Blocks | ID range |')
L.append('|---|---|---|')
for i,r in enumerate(runs,1):
    L.append(f'| {i} | {len(r)} | {r[0]:,} → {r[-1]:,} |')
L.append('')
L.append('Same pattern holds for the other large files (e.g. `german.txt`, `frankish.txt`, `italian.txt`):')
L.append('sorted runs of family/range allocations concatenated as content was added.')

L.append('- `2,601,031` (ex kurdish max): was a typo, fixed to `261031`; kurdish max is now `261,384`-area like its neighbors.')
L.append('')
open('ID_ORDER.md','w',encoding='utf-8',newline='\n').write('\n'.join(L))
print('ID_ORDER.md written, lines:',len(L))

# Character ID ordering & gap analysis

Read-only analysis of block order and ID gaps across `characters/*.txt` (37,700 blocks).
No history files were changed for this report. Method: blocks parsed in file order via `^ID = {`;
comment-aware brace matching; IDs verified unique (see `ID_FIXES.md`).

## TL;DR

- Files are **organized in ascending runs (families/ranges appended in chunks)**, not globally sorted: 82–100% of adjacent ID pairs are ascending, but each file contains several out-of-order chunks.
- Gaps are **huge and harmless**: 37,700 IDs spread over a 0–2.6M space (1.45% dense). The engine only needs uniqueness.
- The `1059xxx–10599xx` cluster and dense `248xxx` block are **deliberate content ranges**, not accidents.
- One gap-causing typo was already fixed: kurdish `2601031` → `261031` (see `ID_FIXES.md` #17).
- Recommendation: leave order and gaps as-is (ID stability matters for saves/tools; sorting would scatter family groups).

## Global picture

- Unique IDs: **37,700** | global min **98** | global max **1,059,999**
- Global span: **1,059,902** → density **3.56%**

### Blocks per 100k ID bucket (global)

| Bucket | Blocks | Bar |
|---|---|---|
| 0-99k | 13,527 | ██████████████████████████████ |
| 100-199k | 13,802 | ███████████████████████████████ |
| 200-299k | 7,913 | ██████████████████ |
| 300-399k |    68 | █ |
| 400-499k | 2,328 | ██████ |
| 500-599k |     4 | █ |

sorted runs of family/range allocations concatenated as content was added.

## Deliberate high ranges (not errors)

- `1,059,xxx–1,059,9xx`: Jade-Dragon-era Tibetan/Chinese/Steppe content (`han`, `sogdian`, `tangut`, `uyghur`, `afghan`, `persian` files).
- `248,xxx`: dense late-avant-garde block shared by many small cultures (daju, kanuri, zaghawa, assyrian, manichean_church, …).
- `2,601,031` (ex kurdish max): was a typo, fixed to `261031`; kurdish max is now `261,384`-area like its neighbors.

`ID_ORDER.md` is done, committed, and pushed — open above.

**Key findings:**
- **Ordering:** files aren't randomly ordered — they're built from ascending *chunks* (82–100% of adjacent pairs ascend). E.g. `english.txt` is 22 sorted chunks (Plantagenets, Courtenays, Umfravilles…) concatenated as content was added. Sorting globally would scatter those family groups.
- **Gaps:** 37,700 IDs over a ~1.06M span (3.56% dense) — very gappy, but harmless; the engine only needs uniqueness. Top-25 gap map is in the report.
- **High ranges are deliberate:** the `1059xxx` cluster is Jade-Dragon-era content, and `248xxx` is a dense shared block for small cultures.
- The 2.6M outlier (kurdish `2601031`) was the typo I already fixed → `261031`.

No history files were touched for this. If you ever *do* want them sorted or compacted, say the word — but my recommendation stands: stable IDs beat tidy IDs here.
metadata.state:
done
